## 🧬 The "Meta-Strategy Permutation Engine" (MSPE)

### Core Architecture

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                         STRATEGY PERMUTATION LAYERS                          │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  LAYER 0: Raw Signals (Your Current Hub)                                     │
│  ├── ML Models (LSTM, XGBoost, Transformer)                                  │
│  ├── Social Scrapers (Reddit, Twitter, TikTok sentiment)                     │
│  ├── Technical Systems (MA crosses, RSI div, etc.)                           │
│  └── On-chain (whale alerts, exchange flows)                                 │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  LAYER 1: Strategy Votes (Individual Systems)                                │
│  ├── System A: "MomentumML" → BUY/SELL/HOLD + Confidence (0-1)               │
│  ├── System B: "SocialSentiment" → BUY/SELL/HOLD + Confidence                │
│  └── System C-N: ...                                                         │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  LAYER 2: Meta-Strategies (Permutation Combinations)                         │
│  ├── Consensus: A=BUY ∧ B=BUY → BUY (Unanimous)                              │
│  ├── Weighted: 0.6*A + 0.4*B > 0.7 → BUY                                     │
│  ├── Sequential: A=BUY → wait 1h → B=BUY → BUY (Cascade)                     │
│  └── Conditional: A=BUY ∧ (B=HOLD ∨ C=BUY) → BUY                             │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  LAYER 3: Symbol-Specific Optimization                                         │
│  ├── BTC: Best = Consensus(ML + On-chain)                                    │
│  ├── ETH: Best = Weighted(Social 0.7 + Tech 0.3)                             │
│  └── SOL: Best = Sequential(Social → ML)                                     │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  LAYER 4: Regime Adaptation                                                  │
│  ├── Trending: Use momentum-heavy permutations                               │
│  ├── Ranging: Use mean-reversion permutations                                │
│  └── Volatile: Use consensus-only (high confidence threshold)                │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 1. Historical Permutation Backtesting System

```python
# permutation_backtester.py - Core Engine
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from itertools import combinations, product
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict, Callable, Optional
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

@dataclass
class Signal:
    system_id: str
    timestamp: datetime
    symbol: str
    direction: int  # -1, 0, 1
    confidence: float  # 0-1
    metadata: dict

@dataclass
class PermutationRule:
    name: str
    logic: Callable[[List[Signal]], Optional[int]]  # Returns -1, 0, 1 or None
    min_confidence: float = 0.5
    lookback_hours: int = 0  # For sequential strategies

class PermutationEngine:
    def __init__(self, symbols: List[str], timeframe: str = '1h'):
        self.symbols = symbols
        self.timeframe = timeframe
        self.systems = {}  # system_id -> historical_signals_df
        self.price_data = {}  # symbol -> ohlcv_df
        self.permutations = []
        self.results = {}
        
    def load_system_signals(self, system_id: str, signals_df: pd.DataFrame):
        """Load historical signals from any system"""
        self.systems[system_id] = signals_df
        
    def generate_permutations(self, max_systems_per_rule: int = 3):
        """Generate all logical combinations of systems"""
        system_ids = list(self.systems.keys())
        
        # 1. Single systems (baseline)
        for sys_id in system_ids:
            self.permutations.append(PermutationRule(
                name=f"SOLO_{sys_id}",
                logic=lambda signals, target=sys_id: self._solo_signal(signals, target)
            ))
        
        # 2. Pairs (AND, OR, XOR logic)
        for sys_a, sys_b in combinations(system_ids, 2):
            # Unanimous BUY
            self.permutations.append(PermutationRule(
                name=f"AND_{sys_a}_{sys_b}",
                logic=lambda s, a=sys_a, b=sys_b: self._and_logic(s, a, b),
                min_confidence=0.6
            ))
            # Either agrees
            self.permutations.append(PermutationRule(
                name=f"OR_{sys_a}_{sys_b}",
                logic=lambda s, a=sys_a, b=sys_b: self._or_logic(s, a, b),
                min_confidence=0.7  # Higher threshold for OR (riskier)
            ))
            # Disagreement = no trade
            self.permutations.append(PermutationRule(
                name=f"AGREE_{sys_a}_{sys_b}",
                logic=lambda s, a=sys_a, b=sys_b: self._agreement_logic(s, a, b)
            ))
        
        # 3. Triplets (majority vote)
        for trio in combinations(system_ids, 3):
            self.permutations.append(PermutationRule(
                name=f"MAJORITY_{'_'.join(trio)}",
                logic=lambda s, t=trio: self._majority_logic(s, t),
                min_confidence=0.5
            ))
            
        # 4. Weighted combinations (if systems have performance history)
        self._add_weighted_permutations(system_ids)
        
        # 5. Sequential/Cascade strategies
        self._add_sequential_permutations(system_ids)
        
        print(f"Generated {len(self.permutations)} permutation strategies")
        
    def _solo_signal(self, signals: List[Signal], target_id: str) -> Optional[int]:
        for sig in signals:
            if sig.system_id == target_id and sig.confidence >= 0.5:
                return sig.direction
        return None
    
    def _and_logic(self, signals: List[Signal], id_a: str, id_b: str, 
                   min_conf: float = 0.6) -> Optional[int]:
        sig_a = next((s for s in signals if s.system_id == id_a), None)
        sig_b = next((s for s in signals if s.system_id == id_b), None)
        
        if not sig_a or not sig_b:
            return None
        if sig_a.confidence < min_conf or sig_b.confidence < min_conf:
            return None
        if sig_a.direction == sig_b.direction == 1:
            return 1
        if sig_a.direction == sig_b.direction == -1:
            return -1
        return 0  # Disagreement = neutral
    
    def _weighted_logic(self, signals: List[Signal], weights: Dict[str, float]) -> Optional[int]:
        """Weighted voting: sum(confidence * weight * direction) > threshold"""
        total_weight = sum(weights.values())
        weighted_sum = 0
        
        for sig in signals:
            if sig.system_id in weights:
                weighted_sum += sig.direction * sig.confidence * weights[sig.system_id]
        
        score = weighted_sum / total_weight
        
        if score > 0.6: return 1
        if score < -0.6: return -1
        return 0
    
    def _sequential_logic(self, signals: List[Signal], primary: str, 
                         secondary: str, delay_hours: int = 2) -> Optional[int]:
        """Primary triggers, secondary confirms within window"""
        # Implementation checks if secondary confirms within delay_hours
        pass

    def run_symbol_specific_optimization(self, symbol: str, 
                                        train_ratio: float = 0.6,
                                        validation_ratio: float = 0.2):
        """
        Find best permutation FOR THIS SPECIFIC SYMBOL
        Uses walk-forward analysis to avoid overfitting
        """
        price_df = self.price_data[symbol]
        n = len(price_df)
        train_end = int(n * train_ratio)
        val_end = int(n * (train_ratio + validation_ratio))
        
        # Align all system signals to price timestamps
        aligned_signals = self._align_signals(symbol)
        
        results = []
        
        for perm in self.permutations:
            # Walk-forward validation
            train_performance = self._backtest_permutation(
                perm, aligned_signals[:train_end], price_df[:train_end]
            )
            
            # Only test on validation if training passed minimum threshold
            if train_performance['sharpe'] > 1.0 and train_performance['trades'] >= 20:
                val_performance = self._backtest_permutation(
                    perm, aligned_signals[train_end:val_end], price_df[train_end:val_end]
                )
                
                # Final test on holdout
                test_performance = self._backtest_permutation(
                    perm, aligned_signals[val_end:], price_df[val_end:]
                )
                
                results.append({
                    'permutation_id': perm.name,
                    'symbol': symbol,
                    'train_sharpe': train_performance['sharpe'],
                    'val_sharpe': val_performance['sharpe'],
                    'test_sharpe': test_performance['sharpe'],
                    'consistency': min(train_performance['sharpe'], val_performance['sharpe'], 
                                     test_performance['sharpe']) / max(train_performance['sharpe'], 
                                                                       val_performance['sharpe'], 
                                                                       test_performance['sharpe'] + 0.001),
                    'total_trades': (train_performance['trades'] + 
                                   val_performance['trades'] + 
                                   test_performance['trades']),
                    'max_drawdown': test_performance['max_dd'],
                    'win_rate': test_performance['win_rate'],
                    'regime': self._detect_regime(price_df)  # Trending/ranging/volatile
                })
        
        # Rank by robustness (consistency across periods)
        results_df = pd.DataFrame(results)
        results_df['robust_score'] = (
            results_df['test_sharpe'] * 0.4 + 
            results_df['consistency'] * 0.3 +
            (1 - results_df['max_drawdown']) * 0.2 +
            np.log(results_df['total_trades']) * 0.1
        )
        
        return results_df.sort_values('robust_score', ascending=False)

    def _backtest_permutation(self, perm: PermutationRule, 
                             signals: List[List[Signal]], 
                             price_df: pd.DataFrame) -> dict:
        """Vectorized backtest for a single permutation"""
        trades = []
        position = 0
        entry_price = 0
        
        for i, (timestamp, row) in enumerate(price_df.iterrows()):
            current_signals = signals[i] if i < len(signals) else []
            
            # Get permutation decision
            decision = perm.logic(current_signals)
            
            # Execute logic
            if decision == 1 and position <= 0:  # Buy signal
                if position == -1:  # Cover short
                    trades.append({'exit': row['close'], 'entry': entry_price, 'type': 'short'})
                position = 1
                entry_price = row['close']
            elif decision == -1 and position >= 0:  # Sell signal
                if position == 1:  # Close long
                    trades.append({'exit': row['close'], 'entry': entry_price, 'type': 'long'})
                position = -1
                entry_price = row['close']
        
        # Calculate metrics
        if not trades:
            return {'sharpe': 0, 'trades': 0, 'max_dd': 1, 'win_rate': 0}
        
        returns = [(t['exit'] - t['entry'])/t['entry'] * (1 if t['type'] == 'long' else -1) 
                   for t in trades]
        
        return {
            'sharpe': np.mean(returns) / (np.std(returns) + 1e-9) * np.sqrt(252),
            'trades': len(trades),
            'max_dd': self._calc_max_drawdown(returns),
            'win_rate': sum(1 for r in returns if r > 0) / len(returns)
        }
```

### 2. The "Phoenix" System: Reviving Failed Strategies

Your insight about **failed strategies becoming winners with additional signals** is crucial. Here's the resurrection engine:

```python
# phoenix_analyzer.py - Failed Strategy Revival System

class PhoenixAnalyzer:
    """
    Analyzes WHEN failed strategies work to create conditional activation rules
    """
    
    def __init__(self, permutation_results: pd.DataFrame):
        self.results = permutation_results
        self.phoenix_rules = []
        
    def find_phoenix_opportunities(self, min_base_sharpe: float = 0.3):
        """
        Find strategies that fail alone but succeed with specific companions
        """
        base_systems = [p for p in self.results if p.startswith('SOLO_')]
        
        for base in base_systems:
            base_perf = self.results[self.results['permutation_id'] == base]
            
            if base_perf['test_sharpe'].iloc[0] < min_base_sharpe:
                # This system "failed" - find what saves it
                base_name = base.replace('SOLO_', '')
                
                # Check all combinations involving this system
                related = self.results[
                    self.results['permutation_id'].str.contains(base_name) & 
                    (self.results['permutation_id'] != base)
                ]
                
                saviors = related[related['test_sharpe'] > 1.5]  # Threshold for "good"
                
                if not saviors.empty:
                    for _, row in saviors.iterrows():
                        # Analyze market conditions when this combo works
                        conditions = self._extract_conditions(row['permutation_id'], base_name)
                        
                        self.phoenix_rules.append({
                            'base_system': base_name,
                            'rescue_combination': row['permutation_id'],
                            'activation_conditions': conditions,
                            'improvement': row['test_sharpe'] / (base_perf['test_sharpe'].iloc[0] + 0.001),
                            'symbol_specific': row['symbol']
                        })
        
        return pd.DataFrame(self.phoenix_rules)
    
    def _extract_conditions(self, perm_id: str, base_system: str) -> dict:
        """Extract market regime conditions where this combination works"""
        # Analyze volatility, trend strength, volume when this perm is active
        return {
            'volatility_regime': 'high',  # Example: only works in high vol
            'trend_strength': 'weak',     # Example: counter-trend works in weak trends
            'volume_percentile': '>70%',
            'time_of_day': 'UTC 14:00-18:00'  # Example: US market open
        }

    def generate_phoenix_strategy(self, rule: dict) -> PermutationRule:
        """
        Create a conditional rule: Activate base only when conditions met + companion agrees
        """
        def conditional_logic(signals: List[Signal], market_state: dict):
            base_sig = next((s for s in signals if s.system_id == rule['base_system']), None)
            if not base_sig:
                return None
            
            # Check if market conditions allow phoenix activation
            if not self._check_conditions(market_state, rule['activation_conditions']):
                return None
            
            # Now check if companion systems agree
            companion_ids = rule['rescue_combination'].split('_')[1:]  # Parse from perm_id
            companions = [s for s in signals if s.system_id in companion_ids]
            
            # Weighted confirmation required
            conf_sum = sum(c.confidence for c in companions if c.direction == base_sig.direction)
            if conf_sum >= 1.0:  # At least one strong or two weak confirmations
                return base_sig.direction
            return None
            
        return PermutationRule(
            name=f"PHOENIX_{rule['base_system']}_{rule['symbol_specific']}",
            logic=conditional_logic,
            metadata=rule['activation_conditions']
        )
```

# .github/workflows/meta-strategy-pipeline.yml
name: Meta-Strategy Permutation Engine

on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 */4 * * *'  # Every 4 hours
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      mode:
        description: 'Run mode'
        required: true
        default: 'update'
        type: choice
        options:
          - update      # Update signals only
          - backtest    # Run full backtest
          - optimize    # Symbol-specific optimization
          - deploy      # Deploy new permutations to live

jobs:
  collect-signals:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v3
      
      - name: Setup Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v4
        with:
          python-version: '3.10'
          
      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          pip install pandas numpy ccxt sqlalchemy gitpython
          
      - name: Fetch Live Signals from All Systems
        env:
          BINANCE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.BINANCE_API_KEY }}
          SOCIAL_SCRAPER_KEY: ${{ secrets.SOCIAL_SCRAPER_KEY }}
          ML_API_ENDPOINT: ${{ secrets.ML_API_ENDPOINT }}
        run: |
          python - << 'EOF'
          import json
          import requests
          from datetime import datetime
          
          signals = []
          
          # 1. Collect from your existing hub API
          hub_response = requests.get('https://findtorontoevents.ca/api/signals')
          for sig in hub_response.json():
              signals.append({
                  'system_id': sig['system'],
                  'timestamp': datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
                  'symbol': sig['symbol'],
                  'direction': sig['signal'],  # -1, 0, 1
                  'confidence': sig['confidence'],
                  'source': 'hub'
              })
          
          # 2. Collect from battleground strategies
          battle_response = requests.get('https://findtorontoevents.ca/battleground/api/picks')
          for pick in battle_response.json():
              signals.append({
                  'system_id': f"battle_{pick['strategy_id']}",
                  'timestamp': datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
                  'symbol': pick['pair'],
                  'direction': 1 if pick['side'] == 'long' else -1,
                  'confidence': pick['score'] / 100,
                  'source': 'battleground'
              })
          
          # 3. Save to signals database
          with open('data/live_signals.json', 'w') as f:
              json.dump(signals, f, indent=2)
          EOF

  run-permutations:
    needs: collect-signals
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v3
      
      - name: Execute Permutation Logic
        run: |
          python permutation_engine.py --mode live --config configs/active_permutations.json

        run: |
          python portfolio_tracker.py --update --notify

    needs: run-permutations
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

      - name: Symbol-Specific Optimization
        run: |
          python optimizer.py --symbols BTC,ETH,SOL --lookback 90d
          
      - name: Phoenix Analysis (Revive Failed)
        run: |
          python phoenix_analyzer.py --min-sharpe 0.5 --generate-rules
          
      - name: Auto-PR for New Permutations
        uses: peter-evans/create-pull-request@v5

          title: "🧬 New Meta-Strategies from Weekly Optimization"
          body: |
            Automated discovery of optimal strategy combinations:
            - Symbol-specific winners identified
            - Phoenix revivals: {{ steps.phoenix.outputs.revived_count }}
            - Consensus shifts detected: {{ steps.optimizer.outputs.regime_changes }}

  auto-deploy:
    needs: performance-analysis

### 4. Portfolio State Management (SQLite + Git)

```python
# portfolio_state.py - Git-tracked portfolio database

import sqlite3
import json
from datetime import datetime
from dataclasses import asdict

class GitTrackedPortfolio:
    """
    Portfolio state that lives in git for transparency and rollback capability
    """
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = 'data/portfolio.db'):
        self.db_path = db_path
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        self._init_tables()
        
    def _init_tables(self):
        self.conn.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS active_permutations (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                symbol TEXT NOT NULL,
                rule_name TEXT NOT NULL,
                systems_involved TEXT,  -- JSON array
                weight REAL DEFAULT 1.0,
                status TEXT CHECK(status IN ('paper', 'live', 'paused', 'retired')),
                activated_at TIMESTAMP,
                performance_snapshot TEXT,  -- JSON
                regime_preference TEXT  -- 'trending', 'ranging', 'volatile', 'any'
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS permutation_signals (
                timestamp TIMESTAMP,
                permutation_id TEXT,
                symbol TEXT,
                direction INTEGER,
                confidence REAL,
                contributing_systems TEXT,  -- Which systems voted
                executed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,  -- Whether trade was taken
                FOREIGN KEY (permutation_id) REFERENCES active_permutations(id)
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS phoenix_revivals (
                base_system TEXT,
                rescue_permutation TEXT,
                activation_conditions TEXT,  -- JSON
                success_rate REAL,
                last_triggered TIMESTAMP,
                total_triggers INTEGER DEFAULT 0
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS symbol_profiles (
                symbol TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                best_permutation TEXT,
                worst_permutation TEXT,
                optimal_regime TEXT,
                last_optimized TIMESTAMP,
                permutation_rankings TEXT  -- JSON: {perm_id: score}
            );
        """)
        
    def commit_state(self, message: str):
        """Commit current DB state to git for version control"""
        import subprocess
        subprocess.run(['git', 'add', self.db_path])
        subprocess.run(['git', 'commit', '-m', f"[PORTFOLIO] {message}"])
        
    def promote_permutation(self, perm_id: str, symbol: str, 
                           from_status: str, to_status: str,
                           reason: str):
        """Graduated promotion with audit trail"""
        cursor = self.conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("""
            UPDATE active_permutations 
            SET status = ?, activated_at = ?
            WHERE id = ? AND symbol = ?
        """, (to_status, datetime.utcnow(), perm_id, symbol))
        
        # Log promotion
        cursor.execute("""
            INSERT INTO promotion_log 
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (datetime.utcnow(), perm_id, symbol, from_status, to_status, reason))
        
        self.conn.commit()
        self.commit_state(f"Promoted {perm_id}/{symbol}: {from_status}→{to_status}")
```

### 6. The "Elimination & Revival" Lifecycle

```python
# lifecycle_manager.py

class PermutationLifecycle:
    """
    Manages the birth, life, and death of meta-strategies
    """
    
    STATES = ['incubator', 'paper', 'live', 'paused', 'retired', 'phoenix']
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.rules = {
            'incubator→paper': self._check_incubator_graduation,
            'paper→live': self._check_paper_validation,
            'live→paused': self._check_live_degradation,
            'paused→phoenix': self._check_revival_potential,
            'phoenix→live': self._check_phoenix_validation,
            'any→retired': self._check_permanent_elimination
        }
    
    def _check_incubator_graduation(self, perm_id: str) -> bool:
        """Require 30 days + 20 trades + Sharpe > 1.0"""
        stats = self.get_stats(perm_id, days=30)
        return (stats['trades'] >= 20 and 
                stats['sharpe'] > 1.0 and 
                stats['max_dd'] < 0.15)
    
    def _check_revival_potential(self, perm_id: str) -> bool:
        """
        Check if failed strategy works in specific conditions (Phoenix)
        """
        # Analyze: Does this work in specific regimes?
        regime_performance = self.analyze_by_regime(perm_id)
        
        # If it works great in high volatility but fails in low...
        if (regime_performance['volatile']['sharpe'] > 1.5 and
            regime_performance['trending']['sharpe'] < 0.5):
            return True
        return False
    
    def auto_transition(self):
        """Run daily state machine transitions"""
        for perm in self.get_all_permutations():
            current = perm['status']
            
            # Check all possible transitions
            for transition, check_func in self.rules.items():
                from_state, to_state = transition.split('→')
                if from_state == current and check_func(perm['id']):
                    self.transition(perm['id'], to_state, 
                                  reason=f"Auto: {transition}")
                    break
```

### 7. Implementation Roadmap

**Phase 1: Foundation (Week 1-2)**
- [ ] Set up SQLite schema with git tracking
- [ ] Implement basic permutation engine (AND, OR, Majority)
- [ ] GitHub Actions pipeline for signal collection
- [ ] Simple backtester with walk-forward validation

**Phase 2: Intelligence (Week 3-4)**
- [ ] Symbol-specific optimization loop
- [ ] Phoenix analyzer for failed strategy revival
- [ ] Regime detection (trending/ranging/volatile)
- [ ] Add 3-4 orthogonal data sources (funding, liquidations, social)

**Phase 3: Automation (Week 5-6)**
- [ ] Auto-promotion/demotion lifecycle
- [ ] Portfolio allocation based on permutation confidence
- [ ] Real-time dashboard showing active permutations
- [ ] Alert system for regime changes

**Phase 4: Meta-Learning (Ongoing)**
- [ ] ML model to predict which permutations will work tomorrow
- [ ] Automated feature engineering from permutation history
- [ ] Cross-symbol learning (BTC patterns → ETH application)

### Key Innovation: The "Strategy DNA" Hash

Track each permutation by a **hash of its component systems** so you can detect when "similar" combinations have worked before:

```python
def get_strategy_dna(systems: List[str], logic_type: str) -> str:
    """Create unique fingerprint for strategy combinations"""
    import hashlib
    dna_string = f"{'|'.join(sorted(systems))}:{logic_type}"
    return hashlib.md5(dna_string.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]

# This allows you to query: "Has any AND combination of ML+Social worked on SOL before?"
```

This architecture turns your collection of systems into a **self-organizing, self-healing meta-strategy** that discovers its own optimal configurations while maintaining full auditability through git-tracked state changes.

I'll provide a comprehensive deep-dive into each component with concrete implementations, database schemas, and architectural patterns.

# data_pipeline/producers/crypto_websocket.py
import asyncio
import websockets
import json
from kafka import KafkaProducer
from dataclasses import asdict

class MultiExchangeWebsocket:
    def __init__(self):
        self.producer = KafkaProducer(
            bootstrap_servers=['localhost:9092'],
            value_serializer=lambda v: json.dumps(v).encode('utf-8')
        )
        self.exchanges = {
            'binance': 'wss://stream.binance.com:9443/ws/!ticker@arr',
            'coinbase': 'wss://ws-feed.exchange.coinbase.com',
            'kraken': 'wss://ws.kraken.com/'
        }
    
    async def binance_handler(self):
        async with websockets.connect(self.exchanges['binance']) as ws:
            while True:
                data = await ws.recv()
                tickers = json.loads(data)
                for ticker in tickers:
                    event = {
                        'exchange': 'binance',
                        'symbol': ticker['s'],
                        'price': float(ticker['c']),
                        'volume': float(ticker['v']),
                        'timestamp': ticker['E'],
                        'bid_ask_spread': (float(ticker['b']) - float(ticker['a'])) / float(ticker['c']),
                        'source_type': 'tick'
                    }
                    self.producer.send('crypto-ticks', event)
    
    async def social_media_scraper(self):
        """Scrape Twitter/Reddit for crypto sentiment"""
        while True:
            # Use Twitter API v2 or Reddit PRAW
            mentions = await self.fetch_social_mentions()
            for mention in mentions:
                sentiment = self.analyze_sentiment(mention['text'])
                event = {
                    'source': mention['platform'],
                    'symbol': self.extract_symbols(mention['text']),
                    'sentiment_score': sentiment['compound'],
                    'volume_mentions': mention['engagement'],
                    'timestamp': mention['created_at'],
                    'influencer_score': mention['follower_count'] / 1000000,
                    'source_type': 'sentiment'
                }
                self.producer.send('social-signals', event)
            await asyncio.sleep(60)  # Scrape every minute

# data_pipeline/consumers/timescale_writer.py
import psycopg2
from psycopg2.extras import execute_values

class TimescaleDBConsumer:
    def __init__(self):
        self.conn = psycopg2.connect(
            "dbname=trading user=postgres password=secret host=localhost"
        )
        self.create_hypertables()
    
    def create_hypertables(self):
        """Create time-series optimized tables"""
        cursor = self.conn.cursor()
        
        # Raw price data
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS crypto_ticks (
                time TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
                exchange VARCHAR(50),
                symbol VARCHAR(20),
                price DECIMAL(18,8),
                volume DECIMAL(18,8),
                bid_ask_spread DECIMAL(10,6),
                PRIMARY KEY (time, exchange, symbol)
            );
            SELECT create_hypertable('crypto_ticks', 'time', if_not_exists => TRUE);
        """)
        
        # Aggregated signals from various systems
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS strategy_signals (
                time TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
                strategy_id VARCHAR(100),
                symbol VARCHAR(20),
                signal_type SMALLINT,  -- -1, 0, 1
                confidence DECIMAL(4,3),
                metadata JSONB,
                PRIMARY KEY (time, strategy_id, symbol)
            );
            SELECT create_hypertable('strategy_signals', 'time', if_not_exists => TRUE);
        """)
        
        # On-chain metrics
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS onchain_metrics (
                time TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
                symbol VARCHAR(20),
                metric_name VARCHAR(50),
                value DECIMAL(18,8),
                exchange_inflow DECIMAL(18,8),
                exchange_outflow DECIMAL(18,8),
                whale_transactions INT,
                PRIMARY KEY (time, symbol, metric_name)
            );
            SELECT create_hypertable('onchain_metrics', 'time', if_not_exists => TRUE);
        """)
        
        self.conn.commit()

### Advanced Signal Combination Logic

```python
# strategies/combination_engine.py
from typing import List, Dict, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
import numpy as np
from scipy.stats import entropy

@dataclass
class Signal:
    source: str
    timestamp: float
    direction: int  # -1, 0, 1
    confidence: float
    timeframe: str  # '1m', '5m', '1h', '1d'
    metadata: Dict

class SignalCombiner:
    def __init__(self):
        self.combination_methods = {
            'unanimous': self.unanimous_vote,
            'weighted_majority': self.weighted_majority,
            'bayesian_fusion': self.bayesian_fusion,
            'dempster_shafer': self.dempster_shafer_fusion,
            'confidence_threshold': self.confidence_threshold,
            'time_decay': self.time_decay_consensus,
            'regime_aware': self.regime_aware_combination
        }
    
    def unanimous_vote(self, signals: List[Signal]) -> Dict:
        """All systems must agree"""
        if not signals:
            return {'signal': 0, 'confidence': 0}
        
        directions = [s.direction for s in signals]
        if all(d == 1 for d in directions):
            return {'signal': 1, 'confidence': min(s.confidence for s in signals)}
        elif all(d == -1 for d in directions):
            return {'signal': -1, 'confidence': min(s.confidence for s in signals)}
        return {'signal': 0, 'confidence': 0}
    
    def weighted_majority(self, signals: List[Signal], 
                         weights: Dict[str, float]) -> Dict:
        """Weighted voting based on historical accuracy"""
        if not signals:
            return {'signal': 0, 'confidence': 0}
        
        weighted_sum = 0
        total_weight = 0
        
        for signal in signals:
            w = weights.get(signal.source, 0.5)
            weighted_sum += signal.direction * w * signal.confidence
            total_weight += w
        
        normalized = weighted_sum / total_weight if total_weight > 0 else 0
        
        if normalized > 0.3:
            return {'signal': 1, 'confidence': min(abs(normalized), 1.0)}
        elif normalized < -0.3:
            return {'signal': -1, 'confidence': min(abs(normalized), 1.0)}
        return {'signal': 0, 'confidence': abs(normalized)}
    
    def bayesian_fusion(self, signals: List[Signal], 
                       prior_accuracy: Dict[str, float]) -> Dict:
        """Bayesian updating of signal probabilities"""
        # Prior probability of price going up (assume 0.5 for simplicity)
        p_up = 0.5
        
        for signal in signals:
            source = signal.source
            accuracy = prior_accuracy.get(source, 0.6)
            
            # Likelihood of signal given actual up move
            if signal.direction == 1:
                p_signal_given_up = accuracy
                p_signal_given_down = 1 - accuracy
            else:
                p_signal_given_up = 1 - accuracy
                p_signal_given_down = accuracy
            
            # Bayesian update
            p_up = (p_signal_given_up * p_up) / (
                p_signal_given_up * p_up + p_signal_given_down * (1 - p_up)
            )
        
        if p_up > 0.7:
            return {'signal': 1, 'confidence': p_up}
        elif p_up < 0.3:
            return {'signal': -1, 'confidence': 1 - p_up}
        return {'signal': 0, 'confidence': 0.5}
    
    def dempster_shafer_fusion(self, signals: List[Signal]) -> Dict:
        """Dempster-Shafer theory for uncertain evidence combination"""
        # Initialize mass functions
        m_buy = 0.3
        m_sell = 0.3
        m_uncertain = 0.4
        
        for signal in signals:
            conf = signal.confidence
            if signal.direction == 1:
                # Combine with buy evidence
                k = m_buy * conf + m_uncertain * conf + m_buy * (1 - conf)
                m_buy = (m_buy * conf + m_uncertain * conf) / k if k > 0 else 0
                m_sell = m_sell * (1 - conf) / k if k > 0 else 0
                m_uncertain = m_uncertain * (1 - conf) / k if k > 0 else 0
            else:
                # Combine with sell evidence
                k = m_sell * conf + m_uncertain * conf + m_sell * (1 - conf)
                m_sell = (m_sell * conf + m_uncertain * conf) / k if k > 0 else 0
                m_buy = m_buy * (1 - conf) / k if k > 0 else 0
                m_uncertain = m_uncertain * (1 - conf) / k if k > 0 else 0
        
        if m_buy > 0.6 and m_buy > m_sell:
            return {'signal': 1, 'confidence': m_buy}
        elif m_sell > 0.6 and m_sell > m_buy:
            return {'signal': -1, 'confidence': m_sell}
        return {'signal': 0, 'confidence': m_uncertain}
    
    def time_decay_consensus(self, signals: List[Signal], 
                            half_life: int = 300) -> Dict:
        """Weight signals by recency (exponential decay)"""
        current_time = max(s.timestamp for s in signals)
        
        weighted_signals = []
        total_weight = 0
        
        for signal in signals:
            age = current_time - signal.timestamp
            weight = np.exp(-age / half_life)  # Exponential decay
            weighted_signals.append(signal.direction * signal.confidence * weight)
            total_weight += weight
        
        consensus = sum(weighted_signals) / total_weight if total_weight > 0 else 0
        
        if consensus > 0.5:
            return {'signal': 1, 'confidence': min(consensus, 1.0)}
        elif consensus < -0.5:
            return {'signal': -1, 'confidence': min(abs(consensus), 1.0)}
        return {'signal': 0, 'confidence': abs(consensus)}
    
    def regime_aware_combination(self, signals: List[Signal], 
                                market_regime: str,
                                regime_performance: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Weight signals based on their historical performance in current regime"""
        regime_weights = {}
        
        for signal in signals:
            source = signal.source
            # Get performance of this source in current regime
            perf = regime_performance.get((source, market_regime), {'sharpe': 0})
            # Convert Sharpe to weight (higher Sharpe = higher weight)
            regime_weights[source] = max(0, perf['sharpe'])
        
        return self.weighted_majority(signals, regime_weights)
```

## 2. Sophisticated Backtesting Framework

### Walk-Forward Analysis with Purged Cross-Validation

```python
# backtesting/walk_forward.py
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from sklearn.model_selection import TimeSeriesSplit
from typing import Tuple, List

class PurgedKFold:
    """
    K-Fold for time series with purging (removing overlapping periods)
    to prevent leakage
    """
    def __init__(self, n_splits=5, purge_gap=10, embargo_pct=0.01):
        self.n_splits = n_splits
        self.purge_gap = purge_gap  # Bars to remove between train and test
        self.embargo_pct = embargo_pct  # % of test to embargo at end
    
    def split(self, X, y=None, groups=None):
        n_samples = len(X)
        indices = np.arange(n_samples)
        fold_size = n_samples // self.n_splits
        
        for i in range(self.n_splits):
            test_start = i * fold_size
            test_end = min((i + 1) * fold_size, n_samples)
            
            # Embargo: remove last % of test set
            embargo_start = int(test_end - (test_end - test_start) * self.embargo_pct)
            
            # Purge gap: remove gap between train and test
            train_end = max(0, test_start - self.purge_gap)
            test_start = min(test_start + self.purge_gap, n_samples)
            
            train_indices = indices[:train_end]
            test_indices = indices[test_start:embargo_start]
            
            yield train_indices, test_indices

class WalkForwardBacktester:
    def __init__(self, data: pd.DataFrame, strategy_class, 
                 train_size=252*5, test_size=63, step_size=21):
        """
        Walk-forward optimization with expanding or rolling window
        
        Args:
            train_size: Bars for training (5 years of daily data)
            test_size: Bars for testing (3 months)
            step_size: How much to advance each iteration (1 month)
        """
        self.data = data
        self.strategy_class = strategy_class
        self.train_size = train_size
        self.test_size = test_size
        self.step_size = step_size
    
    def run(self, param_grid: List[Dict]) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Run walk-forward optimization"""
        results = []
        n_samples = len(self.data)
        
        start_idx = self.train_size
        
        while start_idx + self.test_size <= n_samples:
            train_end = start_idx
            test_end = start_idx + self.test_size
            
            # Split data
            train_data = self.data.iloc[train_end - self.train_size:train_end]
            test_data = self.data.iloc[train_end:test_end]
            
            # Optimize parameters on training set
            best_params = self._optimize_params(train_data, param_grid)
            
            # Test on out-of-sample data
            test_result = self._backtest(test_data, best_params)
            test_result['train_start'] = train_end - self.train_size
            test_result['train_end'] = train_end
            test_result['test_start'] = train_end
            test_result['test_end'] = test_end
            test_result['params'] = best_params
            
            results.append(test_result)
            
            # Advance window
            start_idx += self.step_size
        
        return pd.DataFrame(results)
    
    def _optimize_params(self, train_data: pd.DataFrame, 
                        param_grid: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """Grid search with purged cross-validation"""
        best_sharpe = -np.inf
        best_params = None
        
        for params in param_grid:
            sharpe_scores = []
            
            # Use purged K-fold to prevent leakage
            pkf = PurgedKFold(n_splits=3, purge_gap=5)
            
            for train_idx, val_idx in pkf.split(train_data):
                cv_train = train_data.iloc[train_idx]
                cv_val = train_data.iloc[val_idx]
                
                strategy = self.strategy_class(**params)
                result = self._run_single_backtest(cv_train, cv_val, strategy)
                sharpe_scores.append(result['sharpe_ratio'])
            
            avg_sharpe = np.mean(sharpe_scores)
            if avg_sharpe > best_sharpe:
                best_sharpe = avg_sharpe
                best_params = params
        
        return best_params
    
    def _run_single_backtest(self, train_data, test_data, strategy):
        """Run single backtest iteration"""
        # Fit on training data if needed
        if hasattr(strategy, 'fit'):
            strategy.fit(train_data)
        
        # Run on test data
        portfolio = strategy.run(test_data)
        
        returns = portfolio['returns']
        sharpe = np.sqrt(252) * returns.mean() / returns.std() if returns.std() > 0 else 0
        
        return {
            'sharpe_ratio': sharpe,
            'total_return': (portfolio['value'][-1] / portfolio['value'][0]) - 1,
            'max_drawdown': self._calc_max_drawdown(portfolio['value']),
            'num_trades': len(portfolio['trades'])
        }
    
    def _calc_max_drawdown(self, equity_curve):
        """Calculate maximum drawdown"""
        peak = equity_curve.expanding().max()
        drawdown = (equity_curve - peak) / peak
        return drawdown.min()

# Usage with multiple symbols
class MultiSymbolBacktester:
    def __init__(self, symbols: List[str], data_loader):
        self.symbols = symbols
        self.data_loader = data_loader
        self.results = {}
    
    def run_symbol_specific_optimization(self, strategy_template):
        """Find best strategy parameters for each symbol individually"""
        for symbol in self.symbols:
            print(f"Optimizing for {symbol}...")
            data = self.data_loader.load(symbol)
            
            wf = WalkForwardBacktester(data, strategy_template)
            results = wf.run(self.generate_param_grid())
            
            self.results[symbol] = {
                'best_params': self._extract_best_params(results),
                'performance': results,
                'robustness_score': self._calc_robustness(results)
            }
    
    def run_generic_optimization(self, strategy_template):
        """Find strategies that work across ALL symbols"""
        all_results = []
        
        for symbol in self.symbols:
            data = self.data_loader.load(symbol)
            wf = WalkForwardBacktester(data, strategy_template)
            results = wf.run(self.generate_param_grid())
            results['symbol'] = symbol
            all_results.append(results)
        
        combined = pd.concat(all_results)
        
        # Find parameter sets that work well across symbols
        generic_strategies = combined.groupby('params').agg({
            'sharpe_ratio': ['mean', 'std', 'min'],
            'symbol': 'count'
        }).reset_index()
        
        # Filter for consistency across symbols
        generic_strategies = generic_strategies[
            (generic_strategies[('sharpe_ratio', 'mean')] > 1.0) &
            (generic_strategies[('sharpe_ratio', 'std')] < 0.5) &
            (generic_strategies[('symbol', 'count')] >= len(self.symbols) * 0.8)
        ]
        
        return generic_strategies
    
    def _calc_robustness(self, results: pd.DataFrame) -> float:
        """Calculate how robust the strategy is across different periods"""
        # Coefficient of variation of Sharpe ratios
        mean_sharpe = results['sharpe_ratio'].mean()
        std_sharpe = results['sharpe_ratio'].std()
        
        if mean_sharpe <= 0:
            return 0
        
        cv = std_sharpe / mean_sharpe
        # Lower CV = more robust
        robustness = max(0, 1 - cv)
        return robustness
```

## 3. Meta-Learning & Strategy Resurrection System

### Deep Analysis of Strategy Failures

```python
# meta_learning/failure_analyzer.py
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
import shap

class StrategyFailureAnalyzer:
    def __init__(self, backtest_db):
        self.db = backtest_db
        self.scaler = StandardScaler()
    
    def analyze_failure_modes(self, strategy_id: str) -> Dict:
        """
        Deep dive into when and why a strategy failed
        """
        # Get all trades for this strategy
        trades = self.db.get_trades(strategy_id)
        
        # Separate winners and losers
        winners = trades[trades['pnl'] > 0]
        losers = trades[trades['pnl'] <= 0]
        
        analysis = {
            'temporal_patterns': self._analyze_temporal_patterns(trades),
            'market_regime_breakdown': self._analyze_by_regime(trades),
            'feature_importance': self._identify_failure_features(trades),
            'correlation_breakdown': self._analyze_correlation_changes(trades),
            'drawdown_analysis': self._analyze_drawdown_periods(trades),
            'suggested_modifications': []
        }
        
        # If strategy fails in high volatility, suggest volatility filter
        if analysis['market_regime_breakdown']['high_vol']['win_rate'] < 0.3:
            analysis['suggested_modifications'].append({
                'type': 'add_filter',
                'filter_name': 'volatility_cap',
                'condition': 'skip_if_atr_20d > 2.5 * median_atr',
                'expected_improvement': self._estimate_improvement(trades, 'volatility')
            })
        
        # If strategy fails during trend changes, suggest trend confirmation
        if analysis['market_regime_breakdown']['trend_change']['win_rate'] < 0.2:
            analysis['suggested_modifications'].append({
                'type': 'add_condition',
                'condition_name': 'trend_alignment',
                'rule': 'only_trade_if_adx > 25_and_direction_aligned',
                'expected_improvement': self._estimate_improvement(trades, 'trend')
            })
        
        return analysis
    
    def _analyze_temporal_patterns(self, trades: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:
        """Analyze if failures cluster in time"""
        trades['hour'] = pd.to_datetime(trades['timestamp']).dt.hour
        trades['day_of_week'] = pd.to_datetime(trades['timestamp']).dt.dayofweek
        trades['month'] = pd.to_datetime(trades['timestamp']).dt.month
        
        patterns = {
            'hourly_win_rate': trades.groupby('hour')['pnl'].apply(
                lambda x: (x > 0).sum() / len(x)
            ).to_dict(),
            'daily_win_rate': trades.groupby('day_of_week')['pnl'].apply(
                lambda x: (x > 0).sum() / len(x)
            ).to_dict(),
            'monthly_seasonality': trades.groupby('month')['pnl'].sum().to_dict()
        }
        
        return patterns
    
    def _analyze_by_regime(self, trades: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:
        """Break down performance by market regime"""
        regimes = {}
        
        for regime in trades['market_regime'].unique():
            regime_trades = trades[trades['market_regime'] == regime]
            regimes[regime] = {
                'count': len(regime_trades),
                'win_rate': (regime_trades['pnl'] > 0).mean(),
                'avg_win': regime_trades[regime_trades['pnl'] > 0]['pnl'].mean() if len(regime_trades[regime_trades['pnl'] > 0]) > 0 else 0,
                'avg_loss': regime_trades[regime_trades['pnl'] <= 0]['pnl'].mean() if len(regime_trades[regime_trades['pnl'] <= 0]) > 0 else 0,
                'sharpe': regime_trades['pnl'].mean() / regime_trades['pnl'].std() if regime_trades['pnl'].std() > 0 else 0
            }
        
        return regimes
    
    def _identify_failure_features(self, trades: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:
        """Use ML to identify what features predict failure"""
        # Features at trade entry
        feature_cols = ['volatility', 'trend_strength', 'volume_anomaly', 
                       'rsi', 'macd', 'bb_position', 'correlation_to_btc']
        
        X = trades[feature_cols].fillna(0)
        y = (trades['pnl'] <= 0).astype(int)  # 1 if loss, 0 if win
        
        # Train classifier to predict losses
        clf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=100, max_depth=5)
        clf.fit(self.scaler.fit_transform(X), y)
        
        # SHAP values for interpretability
        explainer = shap.TreeExplainer(clf)
        shap_values = explainer.shap_values(X)
        
        feature_importance = dict(zip(
            feature_cols, 
            np.abs(shap_values[1]).mean(axis=0)
        ))
        
        return {
            'feature_importance': feature_importance,
            'top_risk_factors': sorted(
                feature_importance.items(), 
                key=lambda x: x[1], 
                reverse=True
            )[:3]
        }
    
    def generate_resurrection_strategies(self, failed_strategy_id: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """Create new strategy variants from failed ones"""
        analysis = self.analyze_failure_modes(failed_strategy_id)
        base_strategy = self.db.get_strategy_config(failed_strategy_id)
        
        new_strategies = []
        
        for suggestion in analysis['suggested_modifications']:
            new_strat = base_strategy.copy()
            new_strat['parent_id'] = failed_strategy_id
            new_strat['modification_type'] = suggestion['type']
            new_strat['modification_details'] = suggestion
            
            if suggestion['type'] == 'add_filter':
                new_strat['filters'] = new_strat.get('filters', []) + [{
                    'name': suggestion['filter_name'],
                    'condition': suggestion['condition']
                }]
            
            elif suggestion['type'] == 'add_condition':
                new_strat['entry_conditions'] = new_strat.get('entry_conditions', []) + [{
                    'name': suggestion['condition_name'],
                    'rule': suggestion['rule']
                }]
            
            new_strat['strategy_id'] = f"{failed_strategy_id}_resurrected_{len(new_strategies)}"
            new_strat['expected_improvement'] = suggestion['expected_improvement']
            
            new_strategies.append(new_strat)
        
        return new_strategies

class StrategyResurrectionPipeline:
    def __init__(self, backtester, analyzer):
        self.backtester = backtester
        self.analyzer = analyzer
    
    def run_resurrection_cycle(self, eliminated_strategies: List[str]):
        """
        Full pipeline: analyze failures → generate variants → backtest → promote if improved
        """
        resurrected = []
        
        for strat_id in eliminated_strategies:
            print(f"Analyzing failed strategy: {strat_id}")
            
            # Generate improved variants
            variants = self.analyzer.generate_resurrection_strategies(strat_id)
            
            for variant in variants:
                print(f"Testing resurrected variant: {variant['strategy_id']}")
                
                # Backtest the new variant
                result = self.backtester.run_single(variant)
                
                # Compare to parent
                parent_result = self.backtester.get_result(strat_id)
                
                improvement = (
                    result['sharpe_ratio'] - parent_result['sharpe_ratio']
                ) / abs(parent_result['sharpe_ratio']) if parent_result['sharpe_ratio'] != 0 else float('inf')
                
                if result['sharpe_ratio'] > 1.0 and improvement > 0.2:
                    print(f"✓ Promoting resurrected strategy: {variant['strategy_id']}")
                    variant['performance'] = result
                    variant['improvement_over_parent'] = improvement
                    resurrected.append(variant)
                else:
                    print(f"✗ Variant failed to improve: {variant['strategy_id']}")
        
        return resurrected
```

## 4. Live Portfolio Management & Risk System

```python
# live_trading/portfolio_manager.py
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import numpy as np
from collections import defaultdict

@dataclass
class Position:
    symbol: str
    strategy_id: str
    entry_price: float
    quantity: float
    entry_time: datetime
    side: int  # 1 for long, -1 for short
    current_price: float = 0.0
    unrealized_pnl: float = 0.0
    max_price: float = 0.0
    min_price: float = float('inf')
    
    def update_price(self, price: float):
        self.current_price = price
        self.unrealized_pnl = (price - self.entry_price) / self.entry_price * self.side
        self.max_price = max(self.max_price, price)
        self.min_price = min(self.min_price, price)
    
    @property
    def drawdown_from_peak(self):
        if self.side == 1:
            return (self.current_price - self.max_price) / self.max_price
        else:
            return (self.min_price - self.current_price) / self.min_price

class RiskManager:
    def __init__(self, config: Dict):
        self.max_position_size = config.get('max_position_size', 0.1)  # 10% of portfolio
        self.max_sector_exposure = config.get('max_sector_exposure', 0.3)
        self.max_correlated_positions = config.get('max_correlated_positions', 3)
        self.stop_loss_pct = config.get('stop_loss_pct', 0.05)
        self.trailing_stop_pct = config.get('trailing_stop_pct', 0.03)
        self.max_daily_loss = config.get('max_daily_loss', 0.02)  # 2% of portfolio
        self.var_limit = config.get('var_limit', 0.01)  # 1% daily VaR
    
    def check_new_position(self, portfolio, symbol: str, strategy: str, 
                          size: float, price: float) -> Dict:
        """
        Pre-trade risk checks
        """
        checks = {
            'position_size_ok': True,
            'correlation_ok': True,
            'exposure_ok': True,
            'daily_limit_ok': True,
            'approved': True,
            'reasons': []
        }
        
        # Check position size
        portfolio_value = portfolio.total_value
        position_value = size * price
        if position_value / portfolio_value > self.max_position_size:
            checks['position_size_ok'] = False
            checks['approved'] = False
            checks['reasons'].append(f"Position size {position_value/portfolio_value:.2%} exceeds limit {self.max_position_size:.2%}")
        
        # Check correlation with existing positions
        correlated = self._count_correlated_positions(portfolio, symbol)
        if correlated >= self.max_correlated_positions:
            checks['correlation_ok'] = False
            checks['approved'] = False
            checks['reasons'].append(f"Too many correlated positions ({correlated})")
        
        # Check daily loss limit
        if portfolio.daily_pnl < -self.max_daily_loss * portfolio_value:
            checks['daily_limit_ok'] = False
            checks['approved'] = False
            checks['reasons'].append("Daily loss limit reached")
        
        # VaR check
        var = self._calculate_var(portfolio, symbol, size)
        if var > self.var_limit * portfolio_value:
            checks['approved'] = False
            checks['reasons'].append(f"VaR {var:.2%} exceeds limit")
        
        return checks
    
    def check_exit_signals(self, position: Position) -> List[str]:
        """Check if position should be closed"""
        signals = []
        
        # Stop loss
        if position.unrealized_pnl < -self.stop_loss_pct:
            signals.append('stop_loss')
        
        # Trailing stop
        if position.drawdown_from_peak < -self.trailing_stop_pct:
            signals.append('trailing_stop')
        
        # Time-based exit (if held too long without profit)
        hold_time = datetime.now() - position.entry_time
        if hold_time > timedelta(days=5) and position.unrealized_pnl < 0:
            signals.append('time_stop')
        
        return signals
    
    def _calculate_var(self, portfolio, new_symbol, new_size, confidence=0.95):
        """Calculate Value at Risk using historical simulation"""
        # Get historical returns for portfolio + new position
        returns = portfolio.get_historical_returns(252)  # 1 year
        
        # Simulate adding new position
        new_returns = returns.copy()
        # Simplified: assume new position adds volatility proportional to size
        position_volatility = self._get_symbol_volatility(new_symbol)
        new_returns += np.random.normal(0, position_volatility * new_size, len(new_returns))
        
        var = np.percentile(new_returns, (1 - confidence) * 100)
        return abs(var) * portfolio.total_value

class LivePortfolio:
    def __init__(self, initial_capital: float, risk_manager: RiskManager):
        self.capital = initial_capital
        self.cash = initial_capital
        self.positions: Dict[str, Position] = {}
        self.risk_manager = risk_manager
        self.trade_history = []
        self.daily_pnl = 0.0
        self.equity_curve = [initial_capital]
        self.strategy_allocations = defaultdict(float)
        
    def update_signal(self, strategy_id: str, symbol: str, 
                     signal: int, price: float, confidence: float):
        """
        Process new trading signal
        signal: 1 = buy, -1 = sell, 0 = close
        """
        position_key = f"{strategy_id}_{symbol}"
        
        if signal == 1 and position_key not in self.positions:
            # Open long position
            size = self._calculate_position_size(strategy_id, symbol, confidence)
            
            # Risk check
            risk_check = self.risk_manager.check_new_position(
                self, symbol, strategy_id, size, price
            )
            
            if not risk_check['approved']:
                print(f"Risk check failed for {symbol}: {risk_check['reasons']}")
                return
            
            cost = size * price
            if cost > self.cash:
                print(f"Insufficient cash for {symbol}")
                return
            
            self.positions[position_key] = Position(
                symbol=symbol,
                strategy_id=strategy_id,
                entry_price=price,
                quantity=size,
                entry_time=datetime.now(),
                side=1,
                current_price=price
            )
            
            self.cash -= cost
            self.strategy_allocations[strategy_id] += cost
            
            self._log_trade('OPEN', position_key, price, size)
            
        elif signal == -1 and position_key in self.positions:
            # Close position
            self._close_position(position_key, price, 'signal')
            
        elif signal == 0 and position_key in self.positions:
            # Check risk-based exits even if signal is hold
            position = self.positions[position_key]
            position.update_price(price)
            
            exit_signals = self.risk_manager.check_exit_signals(position)
            if exit_signals:
                self._close_position(position_key, price, exit_signals[0])
    
    def _close_position(self, position_key: str, price: float, reason: str):
        """Close a position and update P&L"""
        position = self.positions[position_key]
        position.update_price(price)
        
        proceeds = position.quantity * price
        pnl = proceeds - (position.quantity * position.entry_price)
        pnl_pct = pnl / (position.quantity * position.entry_price)
        
        self.cash += proceeds
        self.daily_pnl += pnl
        self.strategy_allocations[position.strategy_id] -= position.quantity * position.entry_price
        
        self.trade_history.append({
            'strategy': position.strategy_id,
            'symbol': position.symbol,
            'entry_price': position.entry_price,
            'exit_price': price,
            'pnl': pnl,
            'pnl_pct': pnl_pct,
            'hold_time': datetime.now() - position.entry_time,
            'exit_reason': reason
        })
        
        del self.positions[position_key]
        self._log_trade('CLOSE', position_key, price, position.quantity, pnl, reason)
    
    def update_market_data(self, prices: Dict[str, float]):
        """Update all positions with latest prices"""
        total_value = self.cash
        
        for key, position in self.positions.items():
            if position.symbol in prices:
                position.update_price(prices[position.symbol])
                position_value = position.quantity * position.current_price
                total_value += position_value
        
        self.equity_curve.append(total_value)
        
        # Check for risk exits
        for key in list(self.positions.keys()):
            position = self.positions[key]
            if position.symbol in prices:
                exit_signals = self.risk_manager.check_exit_signals(position)
                if exit_signals:
                    self._close_position(key, prices[position.symbol], exit_signals[0])
    
    def rebalance_strategy_allocations(self, target_allocations: Dict[str, float]):
        """
        Rebalance capital allocation across strategies based on performance
        """
        total_value = self.get_total_value()
        
        for strategy_id, target_pct in target_allocations.items():
            current_value = self.strategy_allocations[strategy_id]
            target_value = total_value * target_pct
            
            if current_value > target_value:
                # Reduce exposure - close some positions from this strategy
                excess = current_value - target_value
                self._reduce_strategy_exposure(strategy_id, excess)
    
    def get_performance_metrics(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate current portfolio performance metrics"""
        returns = pd.Series(self.equity_curve).pct_change().dropna()
        
        return {
            'total_return': (self.equity_curve[-1] / self.equity_curve[0]) - 1,
            'sharpe_ratio': np.sqrt(252) * returns.mean() / returns.std() if len(returns) > 0 else 0,
            'max_drawdown': self._calc_max_drawdown(),
            'current_exposure': 1 - (self.cash / self.get_total_value()),
            'open_positions': len(self.positions),
            'daily_pnl': self.daily_pnl,
            'win_rate': len([t for t in self.trade_history if t['pnl'] > 0]) / len(self.trade_history) if self.trade_history else 0
        }
```

# scripts/run_backtests.py
import ray
import yaml
import click
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

@click.command()
@click.option('--config', default='config/backtest_config.yaml')
@click.option('--parallel', is_flag=True)
@click.option('--workers', default=4)
def main(config, parallel, workers):
    with open(config) as f:
        cfg = yaml.safe_load(f)
    
    if parallel:
        ray.init(num_cpus=workers)
    
    # Load strategies to test
    strategies = load_strategies(cfg['strategy_dir'])
    symbols = cfg['symbols']
    date_ranges = generate_date_ranges(
        cfg['start_date'], 
        cfg['end_date'], 
        cfg['window_size']
    )
    
    orchestrator = BacktestOrchestrator(symbols, strategies)
    results = orchestrator.run_full_backtest(date_ranges)
    
    # Save results
    output_dir = Path('results') / datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    save_results(results, output_dir / 'backtest_results.parquet')
    generate_report(results, output_dir / 'report.html')
    
    # Update database
    update_performance_db(results)

# scripts/evaluate_strategies.py
import click
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
import pandas as pd

@click.command()
@click.option('--eliminate-threshold', default=0.8)
@click.option('--promote-threshold', default=1.5)
def main(eliminate_threshold, promote_threshold):
    engine = create_engine('postgresql://user:pass@localhost/trading')
    
    # Get recent performance (last 30 days)
    recent_perf = pd.read_sql("""
        SELECT strategy_id, 
               AVG(sharpe_ratio) as avg_sharpe,
               COUNT(*) as num_tests,
               SUM(CASE WHEN sharpe_ratio > 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::FLOAT / COUNT(*) as consistency
        FROM backtest_results 
        WHERE test_date >= NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days'
        GROUP BY strategy_id
    """, engine)
    
    # Classification
    to_eliminate = recent_perf[recent_perf['avg_sharpe'] < eliminate_threshold]
    to_promote = recent_perf[
        (recent_perf['avg_sharpe'] > promote_threshold) & 
        (recent_perf['consistency'] > 0.7)
    ]
    
    # Update status
    for _, row in to_eliminate.iterrows():
        update_strategy_status(row['strategy_id'], 'eliminated', 
                             reason=f"Sharpe {row['avg_sharpe']:.2f} below threshold")
    
    for _, row in to_promote.iterrows():
        current_status = get_strategy_status(row['strategy_id'])
        if current_status == 'paper':
            update_strategy_status(row['strategy_id'], 'live',
                                 reason=f"Sharpe {row['avg_sharpe']:.2f}, consistency {row['consistency']:.2%}")
    
    # Generate resurrection candidates
    eliminated = get_eliminated_strategies(days=90)
    analyzer = StrategyFailureAnalyzer(engine)
    for strat_id in eliminated:
        variants = analyzer.generate_resurrection_strategies(strat_id)
        queue_for_backtest(variants)

# scripts/live_update.py
import click
import asyncio
from live_trading.portfolio_manager import LivePortfolio, RiskManager

@click.command()
@click.option('--mode', type=click.Choice(['paper', 'live']), default='paper')
async def main(mode):
    config = load_config(f'config/{mode}_trading.yaml')
    
    portfolio = LivePortfolio(
        initial_capital=config['capital'],
        risk_manager=RiskManager(config['risk'])
    )
    
    # Load active strategies
    strategies = load_active_strategies(mode)
    
    # Main event loop
    while True:
        # Fetch latest data
        prices = await fetch_prices(config['symbols'])
        signals = await generate_signals(strategies, prices)
        
        # Update portfolio
        for signal in signals:
            portfolio.update_signal(
                signal['strategy_id'],
                signal['symbol'],
                signal['direction'],
                signal['price'],
                signal['confidence']
            )
        
        portfolio.update_market_data(prices)
        
        # Log state
        log_portfolio_state(portfolio)
        
        # Check for rebalance
        if should_rebalance():
            allocations = calculate_optimal_allocations(portfolio)
            portfolio.rebalance_strategy_allocations(allocations)
        
        await asyncio.sleep(60)  # 1-minute loop

if __name__ == '__main__':
    asyncio.run(main())

## 6. Database Schema (Complete)

```sql
-- Full database schema for the trading system

-- Strategy registry
CREATE TABLE strategies (
    strategy_id VARCHAR(100) PRIMARY KEY,
    name VARCHAR(200),
    description TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    parent_strategy_id VARCHAR(100) REFERENCES strategies(strategy_id),
    combination_logic JSONB,  -- Stores the signal combination rules
    parameters JSONB,
    status VARCHAR(20) CHECK (status IN ('development', 'paper', 'live', 'eliminated', 'resurrected')),
    elimination_reason TEXT,
    expected_lifetime INTEGER DEFAULT 90,  -- Days before re-evaluation
    version INTEGER DEFAULT 1
);

-- Performance tracking
CREATE TABLE backtest_results (
    result_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    strategy_id VARCHAR(100) REFERENCES strategies(strategy_id),
    symbol VARCHAR(20),
    start_date DATE,
    end_date DATE,
    sharpe_ratio DECIMAL(8,4),
    total_return DECIMAL(10,4),
    max_drawdown DECIMAL(8,4),
    win_rate DECIMAL(5,4),
    profit_factor DECIMAL(8,4),
    num_trades INTEGER,
    avg_trade_duration INTERVAL,
    market_regime VARCHAR(50),
    parameters_used JSONB,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Live trading results
CREATE TABLE live_trades (
    trade_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    strategy_id VARCHAR(100) REFERENCES strategies(strategy_id),
    symbol VARCHAR(20),
    entry_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    exit_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    entry_price DECIMAL(18,8),
    exit_price DECIMAL(18,8),
    quantity DECIMAL(18,8),
    side SMALLINT,
    pnl DECIMAL(18,8),
    pnl_pct DECIMAL(10,6),
    exit_reason VARCHAR(50),
    fees DECIMAL(18,8),
    slippage DECIMAL(18,8),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Portfolio snapshots
CREATE TABLE portfolio_snapshots (
    snapshot_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    total_value DECIMAL(18,8),
    cash DECIMAL(18,8),
    exposure DECIMAL(5,4),
    daily_pnl DECIMAL(18,8),
    open_positions INTEGER,
    strategy_allocations JSONB
);

-- Signal history (for audit and analysis)
CREATE TABLE signal_history (
    signal_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    strategy_id VARCHAR(100),
    symbol VARCHAR(20),
    timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ,
    signal_type SMALLINT,
    confidence DECIMAL(4,3),
    executed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    execution_price DECIMAL(18,8),
    execution_slippage DECIMAL(18,8),
    market_context JSONB  -- Volatility, trend, etc. at signal time
);

-- Failure analysis cache
CREATE TABLE failure_analysis (
    analysis_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    strategy_id VARCHAR(100) REFERENCES strategies(strategy_id),
    analyzed_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    failure_patterns JSONB,
    suggested_modifications JSONB,
    resurrected_strategy_id VARCHAR(100) REFERENCES strategies(strategy_id),
    improvement_achieved DECIMAL(8,4)
);

-- Create indexes for performance
CREATE INDEX idx_signals_time ON signal_history(timestamp DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_trades_strategy ON live_trades(strategy_id, entry_time DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_backtest_strategy ON backtest_results(strategy_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_strategies_status ON strategies(status);
```

## 7. Monitoring & Alerting System

```python
# monitoring/alert_system.py
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Callable
import smtplib
from slack_sdk import WebClient
import asyncio

@dataclass
class AlertRule:
    name: str
    condition: Callable
    severity: str  # 'info', 'warning', 'critical'
    channels: List[str]
    cooldown: int  # seconds

class MonitoringSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.rules = []
        self.last_triggered = {}
        self.slack_client = WebClient(token='xoxb-your-token')
        self.email_server = smtplib.SMTP('smtp.gmail.com', 587)
        
    def add_rule(self, rule: AlertRule):
        self.rules.append(rule)
    
    def check_rules(self, portfolio_state: Dict):
        current_time = time.time()
        
        for rule in self.rules:
            # Check cooldown
            if rule.name in self.last_triggered:
                if current_time - self.last_triggered[rule.name] < rule.cooldown:
                    continue
            
            # Evaluate condition
            if rule.condition(portfolio_state):
                self.trigger_alert(rule, portfolio_state)
                self.last_triggered[rule.name] = current_time
    
    def trigger_alert(self, rule: AlertRule, state: Dict):
        message = self._format_message(rule, state)
        
        if 'slack' in rule.channels:
            self.slack_client.chat_postMessage(
                channel='#trading-alerts',
                text=f"*{rule.severity.upper()}*: {rule.name}\n{message}"
            )
        
        if 'email' in rule.channels and rule.severity == 'critical':
            self._send_email_alert(rule, message)

# Example rules
monitor = MonitoringSystem()

# Rule 1: Large drawdown
monitor.add_rule(AlertRule(
    name='large_drawdown',
    condition=lambda s: s['current_drawdown'] > 0.1,
    severity='critical',
    channels=['slack', 'email'],
    cooldown=3600
))

# Rule 2: Strategy degradation
monitor.add_rule(AlertRule(
    name='strategy_degradation',
    condition=lambda s: any(
        perf['recent_sharpe'] < perf['historical_sharpe'] * 0.5 
        for perf in s['strategy_performance'].values()
    ),
    severity='warning',
    channels=['slack'],
    cooldown=86400
))

# Rule 3: Unusual volume
monitor.add_rule(AlertRule(
    name='unusual_volume',
    condition=lambda s: s['volume_z_score'] > 3,
    severity='info',
    channels=['slack'],
    cooldown=300
))
```

## 8. Advanced Features

### Genetic Algorithm for Strategy Evolution

```python
# optimization/genetic_strategies.py
import random
from deap import base, creator, tools, algorithms

class StrategyGeneticOptimizer:
    def __init__(self, base_strategies, backtester):
        self.base_strategies = base_strategies
        self.backtester = backtester
        
        # DEAP setup
        creator.create("FitnessMax", base.Fitness, weights=(1.0,))
        creator.create("Individual", list, fitness=creator.FitnessMax)
        
        self.toolbox = base.Toolbox()
        self.toolbox.register("attr_bool", random.randint, 0, 1)
        self.toolbox.register("individual", tools.initRepeat, 
                            creator.Individual, self.toolbox.attr_bool, n=len(base_strategies))
        self.toolbox.register("population", tools.initRepeat, list, self.toolbox.individual)
        
        self.toolbox.register("evaluate", self._evaluate)
        self.toolbox.register("mate", tools.cxTwoPoint)
        self.toolbox.register("mutate", tools.mutFlipBit, indpb=0.05)
        self.toolbox.register("select", tools.selTournament, tournsize=3)
    
    def _evaluate(self, individual):
        """Evaluate fitness of a strategy combination"""
        # Decode individual: which strategies to include
        selected = [s for i, s in enumerate(self.base_strategies) if individual[i]]
        
        if len(selected) < 2:
            return (0,)
        
        # Create combined strategy
        combined = self._create_combined_strategy(selected)
        
        # Backtest
        result = self.backtester.run(combined)
        
        # Fitness = Sharpe ratio, penalize complexity
        fitness = result['sharpe_ratio'] - 0.01 * len(selected)
        return (max(0, fitness),)
    
    def evolve(self, generations=50, population_size=100):
        """Run genetic algorithm"""
        pop = self.toolbox.population(n=population_size)
        
        result = algorithms.eaSimple(
            pop, self.toolbox, 
            cxpb=0.7, mutpb=0.2, 
            ngen=generations,
            verbose=True
        )
        
        # Get best individual
        best = tools.selBest(pop, k=1)[0]
        return self._decode_individual(best)
```

This architecture provides:

1. **Scalable Data Pipeline**: Kafka + TimescaleDB for high-frequency data
2. **Sophisticated Signal Combination**: Bayesian, Dempster-Shafer, and regime-aware methods
3. **Robust Backtesting**: Walk-forward with purged cross-validation
4. **Meta-Learning**: Automatic failure analysis and strategy resurrection
5. **Production-Ready Risk Management**: Position sizing, correlation checks, VaR
6. **Full Automation**: GitHub Actions for CI/CD of strategies
7. **Comprehensive Monitoring**: Real-time alerts and performance tracking

The system treats strategies as **evolving hypotheses** rather than static rules, with continuous feedback loops for improvement.

## 🎯 Critical Feedback Round

### 1. **The "Survivorship Bias" Trap in Your Battleground**

Your battleground validates 10 survivors with 8/8 checks, but your permutation engine will inherit their biases. **The problem**: Systems that pass your filters may be overfit to the 2020-2025 crypto bull/bear cycles.

**Fix**: Add **Adversarial Validation** to your permutation layer:
- Before combining System A + System B, check if they failed in *different* historical periods
- If both failed in March 2020 COVID crash → combination is dangerous
- If A failed March 2020 but B succeeded, and vice versa for Nov 2022 FTX → combination is robust

```python
def adversarial_compatibility(system_a_id, system_b_id):
    """Check if systems fail at different times (good) or same times (bad)"""
    a_drawdowns = get_drawdown_periods(system_a_id)  # When it lost >20%
    b_drawdowns = get_drawdown_periods(system_b_id)
    
    overlap = len(set(a_drawdowns) & set(b_drawdowns))
    total_unique = len(set(a_drawdowns) | set(b_drawdowns))
    
    correlation_risk = overlap / total_unique if total_unique > 0 else 0
    
    return {
        'compatible': correlation_risk < 0.3,  # Fail <30% of same periods
        'diversification_score': 1 - correlation_risk,
        'warning': 'High overlap in drawdown periods' if correlation_risk > 0.5 else None
    }
```
